# Dependency audit - 2026-10-07

Started from current main `c7270c8b4ed73c28144abd09dc6811b6ca9d3b50`. Official registry metadata, upstream release tags, runtime schedules and native parent constraints were checked on October 7. The full ledger includes unchanged dependencies, optional/platform artifacts, both ML stacks and resolver-managed hook tooling. Before versions are locked versions; unlocked tooling is labeled separately.

## Source PR map

[PR #392](https://github.com/justinthelaw/justinthelaw/pull/392) is explicitly excluded by the user and remains untouched. There are no other open source PRs to consolidate or close. This isolated branch starts from main; it does not use the separate game checkout.

## Migration and retained constraints

- Transformers 5.19 accepts a fractional warmup through `warmup_steps`, not removed `warmup_ratio`. The previous 5.17 pin has the same incompatibility. Extracted the existing argument construction and added three real constructor regressions (zero, 5% and 100% of 200 steps), plus invalid-ratio validation, first reproduced as TypeError, then passing. CLI `--warmup-ratio` remains available; 100% warmup is translated to the full positive max-steps budget rather than one step. A 100% ratio without an explicit positive step budget is rejected instead of silently configuring one step.
- TypeScript 7.0.2 remains deferred for the website: latest typescript-eslint parser peers require `>=4.8.4 <6.1.0`. Retain latest 6.0.3 until upstream supports 7. [Peer metadata](https://registry.npmjs.org/%40typescript-eslint%2Ftypescript-estree/8.71.1).
- TypeScript 7.0.2 was installed and tested for game tools: the custom CompilerHost requires `ts.sys`, `createCompilerHost`, and virtual vendor resolution. The new native CLI runs but cannot preserve that API boundary; retain 6.0.3 until the supported Compiler API exists. [Upstream API status](https://github.com/microsoft/typescript-go#what-works-so-far). Adjacent note: `tools/pokemon-dungeon/scripts/check-types.mjs`.
- Hub 2.1.1 is now allowed by Transformers and datasets, but tokenizers 0.23.2 still requires Hub `<2`. Retain latest compatible 1.33.0; retry when tokenizers supports Hub 2. Adjacent notes in both requirements manifests. [Tokenizers metadata](https://pypi.org/pypi/tokenizers/0.23.2/json).
- Node 26.11.0 is Current, with LTS scheduled October 28; retain active LTS 24.21.0 and matching Node 24 declarations. Python 3.15 still has no stable final release; retain 3.14.8. [Node schedule](https://github.com/nodejs/Release/blob/main/schedule.json) / [Python tags](https://api.github.com/repos/python/cpython/git/matching-refs/tags/v3.15.0).
- Nano ID 6 and protobufjs 8 remain outside their owning PostCSS/ONNX contracts. Updated the compatible Nano ID 3 line to 3.3.20; retain protobufjs 7.6.6. Parent contracts appear in the ledger.
- npm 12.2 shares [npm/cli #9800](https://github.com/npm/cli/issues/9800): lock-only update rejects optional bundled registry artifacts, while actual update prunes six unchanged Tailwind WASM bundle records. Preserved authoritative records from unchanged Tailwind 4.3.3 and regenerated with native npm. Both clean install and complete virtual dependency graph pass; registry/security defaults and script allowlist remain enforced.
- `braces` 3.0.3 has **no patched published release** for [GHSA-vfj7-8cjw-p6xm](https://github.com/advisories/GHSA-vfj7-8cjw-p6xm). It is inherited through the manual gh-pages publisher (`gh-pages -> globby -> fast-glob -> micromatch -> braces`). npm audit reports five high entries for this single advisory. Latest compatible parents still own the vulnerable version; npm's suggested gh-pages 6.1.1 downgrade removes braces by returning to globby 6 and deprecated/unsupported glob 7, losing newer publisher fixes (including dash-prefixed filenames); current globby is ESM while gh-pages requires CJS. Retain the existing publisher with this explicit deferral; retry on an upstream patch or a separately validated publisher migration. This is not a clean security audit.

## Local validation

- Node 24.21.0/npm 12.2.0 clean native `npm ci`, installed graph and complete `npm ls --all --package-lock-only` pass.
- Website lint, TypeScript and production webpack static export pass. Local flight-check is blocked at Playwright browser download: the proxy truncates Chromium ZIPs, reproduced on baseline and candidate. Browser E2E must be established by current-head CI; no compatibility pin or disabled tests.
- Python 3.14.8/uv 0.12.23 recreate both hash-locked environments (84 training/61 export packages). Both dependency/import checks and exact-lock pip-audit runs pass with no known vulnerabilities.
- Complete training suite: 120 tests pass, including the new real warmup contract. Exporter: four actual offline T5 ONNX tests pass.
- Game tooling static `npm run check` passes (syntax, lint, types, schemas, inventories, manifests and seven vendored files). Native vendor and art exports change only acorn version metadata and source-lock provenance; executable vendor files and all GLBs retain identical bytes. No gameplay/browser execution or deployment.
- Repository contribution hooks pass. GitHub CI and requested Codex review are separate latest-head completion gates recorded on the PR.
